Another spike of pressure. Alastor winced. “Vox,” he growled, “I am very close to—”

“Say it.”

A full three seconds passed. Alastor’s midsection surged, oceans slamming and going to war against once another. Liquid rushed hard against his sphincters. His thighs mashed together, ears flattening just a little. “…Vox,” he muttered.

“Louder.”

“Vox..."

“LOUDER."

Alastor swallowed. "VOX," he gritted out, jaw painfully tight.

Vox leaned in even closer, gripping Alastor's shoulder and giving it a shake. "Yes...? What is it?"

Alastor bristled. "I... I cannot—“

"Gonna have to speak up! Come on, let me hear that pompous radio-announcer voice!"

Alastor shifted, pressure closing in on him, inside and out. He hated this... He didn't think he'd ever felt anything so heinous before. He NEEDED to go, his bladder was BURSTING, urgency slamming into his sphincters. And Vox just kept smirking. "I... Cannot... Relieve myself... while you are listening."

Vox’s face lit up like a glitching fireworks display. “This is the best day EVER,” he shouted, sparks of static flying behind him. “Oh, I’m recording this. I am absolutely recording this.”

Alastor’s blush was faint but unmistakable, a pale red glow beneath his eyes. “You will do no such thing.”

“Go on then, starshine,” Vox teased. “The restroom’s down the hall. Third door on the right. Try not to explode.”

Alastor didn’t dignify that with a response. He simply turned and hurried— Yes, HURRIED—down the hallway, hoping Vox would stay put.

He reached the restroom door. He opened it. He stepped inside and slammed the door, locking it behind him with a decisive click.

The small, pristine room was lit by a single overhead bulb that flickered occasionally, buzzing softly. The tiles were spotless— Of course they were; This was Vox’s domain, and he ran a tight, sterilized ship. Even the sink gleamed.

Alastor exhaled slowly, bracing both hands on the edge of the countertop. “Just a moment of quiet,” he murmured. “A moment of privacy. That is all I require.”

He waited.

The silence was soothing, but the stage-fright remained. The familiar tightening. The stubborn refusal. The humiliating psychological wall of Paruresis rising like a fortress around his nerves.

Alastor grimaced. This internal rebellion was maddening. And it was getting a little dangerous. The pangs were spreading out through his torso now, the sensation he experienced when he probably SHOULD have been having an accident, but the grip of his anxiety stopped the process in its tracks. It became… Blindingly painful after a while, and VERY difficult to conceal.

Thus far, only Charlie had seen him such a state, a couple of weeks after circumstances had forced him to reveal his ‘predicament’ to her. He was at least thankful that she’d been the one there, as he’d LOATHE for anyone else to witness it happening. The worst thing SHE did was fuss over him. She was unsure what would happen to a demon in the event of a bladder-rupture. She knew Alastor wouldn’t be permanently killed by it, but perhaps he’d have to regenerate.

She had gone on and on about some astronomer she’d met while up in Heaven, and how he’d apparently DIED from holding his urine for too long, and how that meant Alastor needed to ALWAYS come tell her if he was having an ‘issue’.

“And, then we’ll just do our little grounding exercise, okay?” Charlie had promised. “For as long as it takes to get you comfortable.”

Of course, Charlie wasn’t HERE now, though. Only Vox. And, there was zero chance that Vox would do a ‘grounding exercise’ with him.

Not that he’d WANT him to.

He took a respectful stance at the toilet, inhaled deeply, and— A faint electronic chirp came from the other side of the door.

Alastor froze.

“Hey!” Vox’s voice called, muffled but unmistakable. “You good in there?”

Alastor’s entire soul curled into itself. “No!” he snapped. “Step away from the door!”

Vox laughed, of course. His amusement had never sounded so irritating before, and that was saying a lot. “Well, excuse me for caring!”

“You do NOT care,” Alastor hissed. “You are merely delighting in my misfortune.”

“Well, yes,” Vox agreed, still chuckling. “I really am.”

Alastor buried his face in one hand.

This was hopeless.

But he wasn’t giving up yet. He straightened, shook out his shoulders, and attempted a relaxation tactic— It was the one Charlie had taught to him on a day that he had fed his coffee addiction far too generously and had then been unable to find a restroom that reached his standards for privacy.

“Deep breaths,” he muttered to himself. “Focus on the environment. Quiet. Stillness. Solitude.”

He tried again.

Nothing.

His bladder tightened further in protest.

He ground his teeth.

“Alright,” he whispered, pacing in place, trying to dislodge the frantic ripples within him. “Think soothing thoughts. Gentle ones. The sound of a forest stream, perhaps. The rush of a calm river. Rain upon—”

“Hey, what’s taking so long?” A speaker crackled within the ceiling— Vox had turned on an intercom. A fucking intercom. WHY was there even an intercom in his bathroom to BEGIN with?!

Alastor’s eye twitched at the sight of it— So violently that several sparks flew out. “Vox!” he shouted. “I do not wish to chat!”

“Oh, okay,” Vox said cheerfully. “Just checking! Because you’re taking a while.”

Alastor gagged on his own indignation. “I am suffering from a medical condition, Vox!”

“That sounds like a skill issue.”

“VOX.”

There was shuffling outside the door, followed by a loud tap-tap-tap. “Heyyy, Alastor?” Vox asked, his voice still amplified through the intercom. “Look, I don’t want to rush you, but this is holding up my schedule. You know, tormenting, broadcasting, meetings— Very important stuff.”

Alastor stared at the ceiling as though begging the universe to strike him down. There was nothing more important to him in this moment than finally emptying himself, his lower abdomen was swollen, tender to the touch— Though, of course, he didn’t dare place a hand against it, having learned already that doing so would only invite more, rougher spasms. But, just the fabric of his coat brushing against it was a terrible tease, the urgent knots that his holding muscles had tied themselves into all twitched and shuddered at the sensation.

He tried again.

Nothing.

The silence inside the room felt too heavy. Too expectant. Too pressurized. The silence in the room meant that there was nothing blocking out Vox’s hearing. He would be aware of every noise that took place inside here. He would Alastor urinate, he would hear each and every drop as it spilled out of him, he would hear how thick and heavy the stream was, how desperate Alastor had been to let it out.

But, of course, he would only hear any of that if Alastor actually MANAGED to let it out, which was looking more and more unlikely by the minute. As had happened to him several other times his stage-fright had taken hold, he couldn’t quite make up his mind as to which was worse; The horrid embarrassment of being heard unloading his bladder, or the terrible pressure of not being able to do so.

The worst part of all, however, was that it was ALSO humiliating to be heard NOT urinating. It was maddeningly contradictory, but having someone— VOX, especially— know that he was in the restroom, not using it, was somehow just as embarrassing as having someone— VOX, especially— overhear his stream.

How could BOTH of those fates be equally as devastating? And shouldn’t he PREFER the outcome where he was listened to while urinating? At least slightly? Since, even if the noise was shameful, an emptied bladder felt much, much better than a swollen, bloated one?

But, no. No matter how many times he tried to reason with himself, the thought of ‘Vox will listen in,’ was far louder, far more persuasive, and far more powerful. And, his floodgates would remain closed. Locked tight.

He swallowed, attempting logic. “This is simply psychological,” he whispered. “I have handled far worse. This is nothing. Absolutely nothing. Just… Let go.”

His body disagreed. It disagreed quite violently. Rather than the relief he so fervently craved, it chose instead to send him volt after volt of electric, pulsing need. His middle was past the point of throbbing or cramping, it was instead alight in constant waves of flaring pressure. There were no moments where the pangs ebbed away or felt more manageable, it was just a nonstop eruption of urgency that seemed to worsen by the second.

He sighed. A soft static pop sounded behind him.

Alastor’s head snapped up in horror.

A tiny wall-mounted screen had flickered to life.

Vox’s face appeared on it, small but unmistakable.

“Before you start yelling,” Vox said immediately, holding up his hands, “I want you to know why this is necessary. See? I just need to make sure you haven’t slipped out the window. Anyway, this is the compromise.”

Alastor stared. The little screen stared back.

Two enemies locked in a battle of wills.

“You are FUCKING LOOKING AT ME,” Alastor said stiffly.

“Yeah, but so what? It’s not like—”

“Avert your gaze.”

A terrible, brittle silence stretched between them before Vox sighed explosively and turned. “There,” he muttered. “Happy?”

“No.”

“Good. That’s consistent.”

Alastor inhaled deeply, determined. He was going to do this. He refused to let Vox be the reason that his bladder detonated. He aimed at the toilet once more, steadying himself.

Focus. Quiet.

He closed his eyes.

Minutes passed.

One minute. Two. Five.

Finally— FINALLY— He felt a slight shift. The beginning of release. A blessed hint of—

A sudden knock-knock-knock rattled against the door. “Aren’t you done yet?” Vox called. “It’s been forever!”

Alastor’s entire nervous system seemed to shut down. He cursed under his breath so viscerally that the lights flickered. He stood perfectly still, every muscle in his body wound tight as piano wire.

“…Vox,” he said with a frightening calm, “I’m afraid that if you knock again, I will be forced to dismantle you molecule by molecule and rearrange you into a decorative lamp.”

“Ooooh,” Vox cooed through the door. “Threats. You must be under a LOT of stress.”

Alastor emitted a sound somewhere between a strangled laugh and a polite scream. He inhaled. He exhaled.

He tried again.

Nothing.

“The situation has become… untenable,” he muttered to himself. “Surely there must be some method— Some strategy— Some trick.”

He frowned thoughtfully. Distraction, perhaps? Or maybe mental imagery? Or—

A soft, extremely unhelpful electronic jingle played overhead from the intercom;“TRY RELAXIIIIING! VoxTek: Trust us with your privacy!”

"Vox!"

"What?" Vox asked. "Just trying to settle you down! If you'd like me to play you something else next time, I can start recording you once you finally decide to piss."

"VOX."

"I could autotune it."

"VOX!" Alastor roared. He didn't even know what the fuck 'autotune' could refer to, but it sounded vaguely nauseating.

“Okay, okay!” Vox yelped through the door. “Sheesh, some demons have no sense of humor!”

Alastor pressed both palms to his temples. ‘Focus. You are the Radio Demon. You can handle this.’ He made another attempt— And immediately froze.

A whirring noise filled the ceiling.

Something shifted behind the fluorescent light panel; A round, mechanical lens descended with a hydraulic hiss. A camera.

“And what is THAT for?!”

“WHAT?!” Vox shouted from outside. “I’m not watching! It’s automated security! And it’s angled at the floor!”

Alastor’s tone went utterly flat. “Angled at the—IT IS POINTED DIRECTLY AT ME.”

“No it’s— Hold on.” Vox tapped something. The camera rotated. Down… Down… Down…

Now it was pointed at the LAST place Alastor wanted it to point.

“…See?” Vox said. “Floor.”

“That isn’t the floor, Vox…” Alastor considered his options, then smacked the camera clean off its mount. It pinwheeled across the ground and skidded to a dramatic stop.

“HEY!” Vox yelled. “That wasn’t free, you know!”

“Good,” Alastor snarled. “Now leave me in peace.”

Vox groaned loudly. “You expect ‘peace’ after that?”

Alastor exhaled slowly— A long, measured breath. He could do this. He COULD. He closed his eyes. Focused. Let the tension drift away like static fading from a channel.

Seconds passed.

A minute.

Two.

A subtle shift, a beginning—

Then—

A soft chime from outside. Ping!

“…Oh, a new message from Valentino,” Vox announced.

Alastor massaged the bridge of his nose. His patience was hanging on by a thread. A small, fraying thread. A thread made of twine. “Vox,” he said slowly, carefully, dangerously, “I require absolute silence out of you.”

“I AM being silent!”

“You are currently arguing with me!”

“YOU started it!”

“VOX.”

“WHAT?”

A beat.

Alastor breathed in. Out. And then came the words he never imagined he would say to anyone— Let alone Vox, the patron saint of irritation. “…If you insist upon standing out there, could you at least make yourself useful and… Provide me with some assistance, rather than further harassment?”

The hallway outside fell so silent Alastor wondered whether Vox’s entire system had crashed.

“…What,” Vox said finally, stunned. “I… Come again?”

Alastor grimaced. “I have attempted every technique I can devise. Grounding. Visualization. Deep breathing. Nothing is working.”

“Well, yeah,” Vox replied, “Because this whole TIME you’ve been stressed, distracted, and yelling at me!”

“That,” Alastor snapped, “Is because YOU have been stress-inducing, distracting, and yell-worthy!”

“Hey, you’re the one that can’t mark his territory like a normal deer.”

“VOX.”

“ALASTOR.”

Another beat.

Alastor inhaled deeply. It felt as though his pride was crumbling like a handful of stale crackers. He glared at the floor like it had personally offended him. The pangs were moving again, beginning to climb upwards, settling inside his back. It was a sharp, stinging pain. Similar, in fact, to being shot— A sensation he was familiar enough with to justify the comparison to himself. It burned, it tore into him, his upper back scorching and searing, like the flesh there was being pulled off in long, bloody ribbons.

His bladder, meanwhile, was like a solid boulder in his core. It pushed and shoved itself against its only proper exit pathway, but couldn’t manage to breach past it. Absolutely nothing came out, he couldn’t even get a hesitant drop to begin collecting onto his tip, and every pulsing throb jolted the fiery fury in his back even more.

He nearly yelped, and holding it back caused the backs of his eyes to burn… No. No. No, no, no. That was worse than shouting in agony would be! Even if he WAS alone in the room, nobody present to see it, he could NOT let a tear slip out over THIS.

‘It’s just a minor physical discomfort, just a small, biological hiccup…’ Alastor told himself. ‘Nothing devastating. Nothing—‘

All of his nerve endings seemed to shriek simultaneously, pressure exploding out from the angry, surging ball of liquid in his center and electrifying every nook and cranny within him. The all-consuming need to just let GO— Even just a little!— Burst up his back, crushed his sides, slammed through his chest… He felt desperate all the way up to his ears, which were pressed firmly against his head. A gnarled huff of air escaped his lips, the closest thing he could ever allow to a cry of pain.

Outside, he heard Vox pause. Then sigh. “How about some… Ambient noise? A nice, relaxing rainstorm? A noise wall? Something to cover up the sounds?”

Alastor hesitated. “… That would be helpful.”

“Alright!” Vox said brightly. “That I can do! I’m a master of noise!”

“That,” Alastor muttered, “Is the problem.”

“No, no— Trust me,” Vox said. “I’ve got this. Just sit tight.”

“Standing,” Alastor corrected automatically.

“Whatever.”

A moment of quiet. Then— A crackle. A hum. A soft, rising swell of audio—

“NOW FOR THE TOP TEN CREEPY SOUNDS YOU CAN’T EXPLAIN—”

“VOX!” Alastor snapped, the force of his own voice adding another pound of pressure to the load already building up inside him.

“IT WAS A POP-UP AD!” Vox yelled back. “I’M FIXING IT! ONE SEC—”

Static.

Then the sound abruptly shifted to; “WELCOME BACK, TODAY’S HEADLINES—”

“NO!” Alastor yelled. “I DO NOT WANT THE NEWS! TURN IT OFF!”

“I’M TRYING!”

The audio flickered again; “HERE ARE THE TOP RESULTS FOR ‘HOW TO PEE QUIETLY—’”

“WHY WOULD YOU SEARCH FOR THAT?!” Alastor demanded.

“I DIDN’T!” Vox cried back. “IT WAS AUTOCOMPLETE!”

“WHAT THE FUCK IS AUTOCOMPLETE?!”

Finally, the audio stabilized; Calming wind. Distant, peaceful rainfall. A faint, soothing ambiance like a tranquil forest at dusk.

Alastor blinked. “Oh my… That is surprisingly pleasant.”

“I know, right?” Vox said proudly. “It’s a soundscape! No voices. No beats. Just background noise.”

“This may… Actually help,” Alastor admitted.

“Yeah, yeah, don’t faint from gratitude.”

Alastor relaxed his shoulders. Inhaled. Exhaled. The soundscape rippled, gentle and reassuring. He closed his eyes and let the tension drift… Stillness. Quiet. Privacy.

And— At last— A subtle, precious shift. A tension lifting. A weight gone. A breath released. But, that wasn’t the only thing he released; A strong stream began to pour, his bladder suddenly going from completely locked-down to gushing as forcefully as was physically possible, desperate beyond all reason to get everything out now that the path to freedom was finally opened. A quiet, triumphant whisper escaped him; “…Hallelujah.”

It just kept coming and coming, the amount was surprising even to Alastor himself. In spite of the intensity of the urge, the severity of the aches he’d endured, he could hardly believe he’d had so much trapped inside of him. No wonder he’d almost…

Well, he HADN’T teared up. THAT was what truly mattered. That, AND the fact that he was actually going, that his agony had reached its blissful conclusion at last, that… That Vox hopefully couldn’t HEAR the thunderous roar of his liquids as they pounded into the bowl. It was… Awfully loud, the result of having so much pressure built up behind the flow…

But, the ambient noise Vox had put on was still playing… That… Surely that covered it up… If not COMPLETELY, then at least most of the way?

He stood still for a moment after he was finally done, allowing the victory— Absurd as it was— to wash over him. He had done it. He had conquered the impossible; He had relieved himself in Vox’s bathroom.

And it had only required one full hour’s worth of pure, misery-inducing psychological and physiological torture!

How simple!

He straightened his coat, fixed his bowtie, and opened the restroom door.

Vox was there, staring at him. “…Well?” Vox said.

Alastor gave a graceful, gentlemanly bow. “Success,” he announced.

Vox blinked. “…FINALLY?! Took you long enough!”

“Indeed. I feel quite refreshed,” Alastor said. “Now then,” he continued cheerfully, “Shall we resume the business of tormenting one another in more dignified, civilized ways, then?”

Vox only grinned. He had something very different planned. “Back in the chair,” he said.

Alastor hummed to himself, the twitch of his eyelid the only thing betraying his irritation. And, beyond that, his… Worry. He was actually worried. He knew when Vox was planning something, and all the signs were there now, clear as day.

His nemesis now knew of his humiliating weak-spot. Nothing pleasant could possibly come from that.

But, his grin broadened. Whatever was coming, he’d keep control. He’d smile through it.

***

The static hum of Vox’s studio purred like a living thing.

Banks of monitors stretched from floor to ceiling, each one glowing with cyan light and flickering with endless advertisements, stolen footage, and looping animations of Vox’s own smug grin. The screens reflected as angular shards across the polished chrome floor, making the whole space feel like standing inside a shattered mirror.

At the center of it all lounged Vox himself. His screen-face buzzed with anticipation, a jitter of electric pixels forming a grin too wide to be friendly.

“Oh-ho-ho,” he chuckled, tapping manicured fingers together. “This is going to be prime-time gold, Vees.” He didn’t even look at his cohorts. Velvette sat atop a stack of speakers, kicking her legs idly while checking her phone, and Valentino lounged on a neon couch, smoking with bored elegance. “Hell is going to EAT. THIS. UP.”

Behind Vox, tied down in the chair once more, was Alastor. Though his grin remained intact, the shadows beneath his eyes were deeper, his posture too rigid, his smile a little too careful. His voice, when he spoke, carried that unmistakable warm, old-time radio cadence— But thinner.

“You do seem remarkably confident.”

“Confidence?” Vox barked a laugh, pixels flaring. “Oh, sweetheart, I’m thrilled. Because today…” He gestured grandly, and the monitors around the room lit up with a countdown. 00:00:10. “I get to give Hell something they’ve NEVER seen.”

Velvette finally looked up from her phone, grinning. “You’re seriously doing a broadcast? Right now?”

Vox didn’t answer, he was too busy smoothing imaginary wrinkles from his jacket, lifting his chin as the countdown neared zero.

Three.

Two.

One.

Every screen in Hell went to static. Every billboard, every phone, every television, each one flickered before stabilizing on Vox’s grinning face. Citizens paused mid-argument, mid-sin, mid-stride. The world of the damned froze.

“Well hellooooooo, sinners!” Vox chimed, voice booming from a thousand speakers at once.

A camera swung to center on him, framed immaculately as he rose from his chair. “I’ve interrupted all programming today to bring you a very special announcement.” His smile widened, glitching slightly. “You all know Alastor—The Radio Demon. Mister Mysterious. Mister Untouchable. Mister ‘I’m-so-terrifying-no-one-better-dare-cross-me.’” He paced forwards, the camera following. “I’ve still got him prisoner.”

Monitors across Hell lit with footage; Alastor, bound to the chair.

“Oh yes. But that’s not even the best part,” Vox crooned. “See, folks… During his captivity, I happened to discover that our dear Alastor has a little problem. Something he’s been very good at hiding.”

Alastor’s grin stiffened. He was not at all SHOCKED by the subject matter of Vox’s announcement. His rival was nothing if not predictable. There was a REASON his recurring nightmares had always featured a scene like this; He KNEW, deep in his bones, how Vox would respond to nearly any given situation.

Even so, the racing thoughts had arrived in full force; ‘No one can ever know!’ “Vox—”

“No, no, don’t interrupt,” Vox said. “You’ve kept this secret for decades. But I think we all deserve a little transparency from our favorite boogeyman.”

Velvette’s face split into a mischievous smirk. Valentino actually sat forward, intrigued.

Vox leaned in towards the camera. “Alastor,” he announced, “Suffers from something he calls Paruresis.”

Silence.

“...What?” Velvette asked.

Vox clapped his hands, delighted. “Paruresis! Also known as shy bladder syndrome. Alastor can’t pee if someone’s watching. Or even nearby. Isn’t that just adorable? Terrifying Overlord Alastor, brought down by stage fright!”

Alastor’s smile remained— But the static around him wavered, his aura twitching. This was like something out of a nightmare. Would Vox be believed? Most likely, it wasn’t as though Alastor was capable of proving him wrong. Any attempt he made to do so would obviously just confirm Vox’s claim.

“… Your fascination with my bodily functions is endlessly juvenile,” Alastor said smoothly. “Though I suppose one must play to their audience.”

“Oh, you don’t get to wriggle out of this with fancy talk.” Vox’s voice crackled with glee. “No wonder you leave meetings early…“

Alastor’s cheek twitched— The subtlest micro-expression, but enough to send Vox over the moon with triumph.

“Ohhh, that got you, didn’t it?” Vox crooned. “See, people? Even powerful monsters have weaknesses. Some Overlords enslave thousands. Some command armies. And some…” Vox flung an arm toward Alastor, “… Can’t take a piss.”

“It is a medical condition,” Alastor replied calmly. “A very common one, I might add. I’m quite flattered by how much you’ve studied my bathroom habits, Vox. That level of obsession is usually reserved for fans.”

***

Charlie froze mid-gesture with a broom in hand. She had been sweeping confetti from an earlier “improvised musical number” (Niffty’s words), when every single screen in the hotel suddenly locked into Vox’s broadcast.

Charlie’s eyes widened to full dinner-plate size when she heard him say the words “Alastor” and “Paruresis.”

“Wh—What?!”

The broom clattered to the floor.

Across the room, Vaggie whipped around with a glare sharp enough to slice drywall. “What did that egomaniac just say?”

Angel Dust, halfway through eating a questionable dessert Husk had dared him to try, snorted powdered sugar out his nose. “Uh oh, this ain’t gonna be good…”

On the television, Alastor appeared—bound, still smiling, still performing calm despite the restraints and the citywide attention.

Charlie pressed her hands to her mouth. “Oh no. Oh no no no. This is—this is bad! This is so, so, SO bad! Poor Alastor!”

Husk leaned over the bar, squinting. “Ah, shit… Guess sometimes dreams DO come true.”

“Fuck!” Charlie gasped. “And after I PROMISED that I wouldn’t let Vox find out!”

“He’s Vox’s prisoner now,” Husk muttered. “What could you have even done?”

“I should have done SOMETHING!” Charlie crossed her arms tightly. “This is humiliation. Live. Public. And with something medical.” Her scowl deepened. “It’s cruel. This is wrong on so many levels. He’s suffering!” Charlie gestured frantically at the screens. “Look at him! He’s trying to play it off, but I KNOW that smile. That’s his ‘I’m silently imploding but maintaining polite composure’ smile!”

Angel blinked. “That’s a thing?”

“Yes!” Charlie pointed at the monitor. “Look! The cheek twitch. The corner tightening. And his shoulders— He holds tension right there when he’s anxious.”

Everyone stared.

The screen showed Alastor with perfect posture, perfect smile, perfect composure— Only if someone knew exactly what to look for would they see the subtle telltale signs of internal turmoil; Charlie knew.

Vaggie sighed. “…She really does pay too much attention.”

“Is that supposed to be a BAD thing?!” Charlie flailed.

“No, babe, it’s adorable. Terrifying, but adorable.”

***

Vox pivoted dramatically towards the camera, the movement sharp enough to produce a crackle of blue lightning. “See, folks, this is what I LOVE about broadcasting!” he shouted. “You can take the scariest monster in Hell and show everyone how human he really is! And humiliation is the purest entertainment.”

Alastor’s smile faltered—but only for a fraction of a second. A flicker. A glitch.

Vox caught it instantly.

“Oh-ho-ho, there it is!” he crowed. “There’s that delicious little twitch! I knew that would get to you!” He wagged a finger in front of Alastor’s face, tutting. “You pretend you don’t care. But you do. Because you’ve built your entire reputation on mystery. On nobody knowing your weaknesses. And now?” Vox spun with a flourish, arms wide. “Every demon in Hell is picturing you frozen in a public restroom.”

Velvette burst out laughing. Valentino chuckled, low and smoky.

Alastor stayed still, shoulders pulled back, voice tranquil as a lake at dawn. “Are you finished?” he asked.

Vox’s pixels flickered into an expression that clearly meant ‘not even close.’ “My dear Alastor,” he said, placing a hand over his chest in mock sincerity, “We’re only in the first act. I haven’t even gotten to the good part.”

The restraints around Alastor pulsed, tightening. Alastor winced— Just a little— And Vox lit up like a child on Christmas morning. “Now, now,” Vox said cheerily, “Don’t get shy on me. I mean, more than usual.”

Alastor’s eyes narrowed, ruby glow deepening.“You seem dreadfully invested in this,” he murmured. “One might wonder why.”

“Oh, that’s easy,” Vox said. “Because I’m having FUN.” His laughter crackled through every speaker in Hell.

“This is the first time you’ve ever been vulnerable in front of me,” he continued. “And frankly? I’m savoring it.” He leaned in so close that the glow from his screen-face reflected in Alastor’s eyes.“You pretend you’re above embarrassment. Above basic needs. Above everyone.” Vox’s voice dropped to a silky, electric murmur. “But now I know what gets under your skin.”

“Do you?”

Vox grinned back, a thousand pixels sharpening like teeth. “Oh, absolutely,” he said. “And I’m going to make sure the entirety of Hell knows it, too.” He turned to the camera, posture instantly bright and animated again.

“Do you want to know the best part?” he clapped his hands. “A mere hour ago, he was so desperate that he asked ME for help. Huffing in pain until I finally gave in and played him some running water sounds. You should have been there to see it… Oh, but I guess that would have made it even HARDER for poor Alastor, wouldn’t it?” He laughed harder.

Alastor stared back at him, unblinking.

Flashes of static danced off of him uncontrollably.

There were plenty of things Alastor could play off, plenty of situations he could extract himself from. This was not one of them. He did NOT know what to do now. His nightmares were coming true, and it was all he could do to just smile through it…

So, a warning for sexual assault for this chapter. Val is in Hell for a reason.

***

The following few days were some of the most infuriating Alastor had ever experienced. He remained tied to his chair, mocking Vox whenever he could, but when it came time for him to tend to his needs, he was forced to ask Vox to assist him. He was forced to tell Vox when he was thirsty or hungry, then allow the bastard to hold a straw up to his lips or spoon-feed him, since he refused to untie him just for that.

Sometimes, Vox would refuse until Alastor had phrased the request in a specific way. “Say ‘please,’ Alastor. Say ‘Please, may I have a drink, Master Vox?’

Alastor would grit his teeth, remind himself that this was all necessary for his plan to work, and repeat the statement.

The worst was, of course, when he needed to relieve himself. Getting Vox to untie him was always a process. Threatening to piss on the floor didn’t work after that first time, not once Vox knew it was a bluff Alastor couldn’t actually follow through on. So, instead he’d have to say ‘Please’ and ‘I can’t wait any longer, Vox’ as Vox laughed his ass off.

Once Vox finally gave in and untied him, he’d follow Alastor to the restroom and wait right outside the door, finding new ways to torment him and invade his privacy until he finally grew bored of it and played the running water noises Alastor needed in order to let himself go.

Through it all, the announcement Vox had made to the denizens of Hell replayed itself in an endless loop within Alastor’s head. Everyone knew now. Literally EVERYONE. And, after he’d already allowed his reputation to take a hit by getting captured by Vox.

He knew that, once his plan was finished and he’d broken free from Vox, his status would be repaired a bit, but how much?! When everyone knew that he couldn’t… That he struggled with something as simple as urination?!

How did he even BEGIN to fix that?!

Most of the jabs he kept directing at Vox were focused on Vox’s need for the other Vees, on how powerless Vox would be without the two of them, on how pathetic Vox was for relying on anyone other than himself.

Yet, there Alastor was, relying on Vox to be able to fulfill his most basic needs. And, beyond that, he relied on Charlie to help him relieve himself. THAT was much more pathetic than Vox’s relationship with the Vees, wasn’t it?

Perhaps, that was why Alastor found himself making more and more aggressive comments about it to Vox any chance he got, the more he mocked VOX for his silly dependency, the less Alastor had to acknowledge his own.

At least Vox hadn’t found out about that deal with Charlie. And, if Alastor made it clear enough to him that he never, EVER needed anyone, he wouldn’t even EXPECT it.

A couple times, Alastor would reach his limit when Vox wasn’t even around to untie him. He’d have to… Ugh… Rely on one of the other Vees to let him go.

Velvette, at least, wasn’t SO terrible about it.

She DID snort to herself when she noticed him fidgeting. “Pfft… I’d better untie you before you stress-test your… Plumbing.”

Alastor inhaled sharply through the nose. “My dear, that phrasing is entirely unnecessary.”

“Awwww, look who’s sensitive when he’s desperate.” Velvette bent down behind him, inspecting the knots with exaggerated care. “Whew! You really are trussed up. Bet this is the longest you’ve ever sat still.”

Alastor stiffened. “Do hurry.”

She clicked her tongue. “Fine, fine. Let’s see… Ah! There we are.” She loosened his wrists, setting them free. Alastor flexed them immediately, relief flickering across his face— Though his posture remained rigid, careful.

As Velvette moved to his ankles, her expression softened just a hair— Barely noticeable unless one knew her well. “I’m not actually trying to torture you, y’know,” she said, kneeling as she fiddled with the cable. “I just like messing with you. It’s kinda my thing.”

“I gathered,” Alastor replied, voice tight but genuine. “And while your sense of humor is… Distinct… I appreciate the assistance.”

“Whoa,” Velvette glanced up. “Was that a compliment?”

“Do not let it go to your head.”

She grinned, finishing with the rest of the restraints.

Alastor was on his feet instantly— Though carefully, VERY carefully— Straightening his coat and adjusting his bowtie with the precision of a man desperately assembling his dignity brick by brick.

Velvette rose as well, stretching. “Man, you’re tense. You look like you’re afraid to move too fast or you’ll—”

He cut her off sharply. “Let us NOT finish that sentence.”

Velvette giggled. “Right, right.” She gestured grandly toward the hallway. “Go on.”

“Thank you,” Alastor said, already heading for the door with a speed that somehow still looked like a gentleman’s brisk walk rather than a sprint. He soon came to an abrupt stop. “You’re… Not following me.”

“Vox’s little announcement gave the impression you wouldn’t LIKE that,” Velvette said.

“I’m just… Surprised,” Alastor said, resuming his walk.

Velvette called after him, “Want me to clear the hallway cameras so this isn’t, like, immortalized forever?”

Alastor paused mid-step. Slowly. Very slowly. He turned his head. “…Yes,” he said. “If you would.”

Velvette smirked triumphantly. “See? I CAN be nice.”

Alastor offered her a strained but sincere nod. “You have my gratitude.”

Then he vanished down the corridor, radio static trailing behind him like a fraying thread.

Velvette folded her arms, watching him go, a mischievous but oddly fond smile tugging at her lips. “Damn,” she murmured to herself. “He’s kinda funny when he’s flustered.”

Having HER untie him wasn’t torturous, Alastor decided. But, Valentino, on the other hand…

Valentino teased him a LOT more, finding the situation almost as hilarious as Vox did. Or, at least, Alastor THOUGHT he was finding it funny… It was obvious that Valentino was thoroughly entertained by it, but the strange thing was that he didn’t laugh. He just… Taunted Alastor and couldn’t keep his eyes off of him.

It must have been amusement, surely? Even if he wasn’t snickering?

“Mm, look at you,” Valentino said. “You’re so FULL… You must be ready to pop…”

Alastor tried to keep himself still, the restraints helped with that a bit, but Valentino was right; Alastor WAS ready to pop, and his body shifted involuntarily, as much as it could.

“Can’t even cross your legs,” Valentino cooed. “Poor thing…”

Alastor’s ears flattened and he forced a chuckle, tuning his voice to a warm AM broadcast. “I do fear your hospitality leaves something to be desired. Your chairs lack… Amenities.”

Valentino nodded. “Ohhh, I know. You’re completely helpless. Completely desperate…”

If the words hadn’t been enough to make Alastor flinch, the way Valentino licked his lips certainly was. He had been… Concerned about one of the Vees taking advantage of his captivity to…

He shut his eyes. No. Not that. He’d endure any form of humiliation and pain BUT that. His plan wasn’t worth THAT. Please…

Vox had already been putting his hands on him since his imprisonment began.

Vox had decided he just HAD to know if Alastor had a tail or not, and he’d refused to just take Alastor’s word for it when he said he didn’t. No, he’d had to check. Thoroughly. First he’d groped Alastor’s posterior, ignoring all the shrieks and squeals of radio feedback Alastor couldn’t control, all the distress signals screaming at Vox to stop touching.

But, even that wasn’t enough. Vox claimed he still couldn’t tell for sure if there was a tail or not. Then, before Alastor could respond, he’d torn Alastor’s pants down, exposing him down to his knees. Alastor had quickly shielded his front, but Vox’s gaze still lingered on his backside for so long— Way longer than it would have taken to realize there really wasn’t a tail.

And then, later, he’d forced Alastor to watch as he and Valentino engaged in that revolting, sweaty activity.

But please… Please, no further than that…

To his immense relief, Valentino made no move to touch him. He simply continued to watch with that unwavering gaze. “I wish Vox would let me take you to the studio… This would be amazing content. I could make you a star.”

Alastor shook his head quickly. No, not there… The image of himself forced onto a bed, hands all over him… Things… Inside him… “I am already a star,” he said, struggling to keep his voice stable. Static popped on every word.

“Oh, but you’d be better at this content than Angel is,” Valentino said.

If Alastor’s ears went any flatter, they’d no longer be visible beneath his hair.

“He would have already pissed himself by now,” Valentino continued. “But, you… You just keep holding it. And, if you literally CAN’T go with the camera rolling, we could record a whole new level of desperation.”

Alastor didn’t quite understand. It, at least, didn’t sound like Valentino wanted to violate him, but recording him in this compromising position, keeping a camera fixed on him for who-knew-how-long so that he couldn’t possibly go… That was STILL horrible, just far from the WORST thing Valentino could do to him.

“A bit of comfort would be appreciated.” Alastor fidgeted again, trying subtly to ease the pressure building inside him. It didn’t work. “Just untie me for a moment…”

Valentino laughed, leaning against the wall. “And why would I do that?”

Alastor’s smile didn’t budge, though it felt stretched thinner than usual. “You know why. So, if you would be so kind as to untie me, we can avoid a situation that neither of us particularly wants.”

Valentino tilted his head. “Who says that ‘situation’ ISN’T what I want?”

Alastor tried to press his knees together, which was difficult in his bindings. What? The fuck? If Alastor DID actually… Burst, he assumed Vox would make Valentino clean it up, why would— Oh, fuck, what if Vox made Valentino clean HIM up? Was this a ploy to get Alastor alone in the bath with him?! Free to touch him, to— “Untie me. Now.”

Valentino tapped a finger on his lip, clearly enjoying the leverage far too much. “Hmmm… Giving me orders while you’re tied up? Bold of you.”

Alastor’s eyes flashed a subtle crimson— Nothing overt, nothing threatening, but enough to hint at what might come should the ropes not be removed before his patience was fully spent. “It isn’t an order,” he said softly. “It’s a practical suggestion. I believe we can both agree that I am far more agreeable when not… Uncomfortable.”

Valentino hesitated.

For the first time, Alastor’s smile faltered just enough to betray the urgency and fear that he was trying desperately to hide. He pressed his knees together, adjusting carefully to keep control.

“I could get you a bottle,” Valentino said.

“I’m sure you can tell that I don’t desire a DRINK now.”

“Not to drink,” Valentino corrected. “An empty one.”

It took Alastor a moment to catch on. His ears flattened and his face darkened just a little. “And, HOW,” he flexed his arms as much as he could. “Do you propose I do THAT? I don’t exactly have access to my hands…”

He should have known better than to ask that. Should have known what Valentino had in mind.

“Ah, but I can use ALL of mine.”

No. Nope. No, no, no. NO ONE else was permitted to unzip him, and then… Valentino’s disgusting, perverse hands… Grasping hold of Alastor’s— He shook his head back and forth several times, eyes scrunched closed.

Valentino snickered. “Aw, is our little radio star afraid of a little fondling?”

Alastor’s anxiety and desperation climbed up from his bladder and into his chest like a vine. “Did you forget Vox’s little… Announcement so soon? I require privacy.”

“If you need to go badly enough, it will happen eventually,” Valentino said.

That was NOT the case for Alastor… He could already feel a few warning jabs in his lower back, reminding him of exactly what was bound to happen to him instead.

“And, if you need help relaxing…” Valentino fluttered his many hands. “We could have a little extra fun after I get your zipper down… It’s easier to hold it if you’re hard, you know.”

Alastor’s twitching froze. His bladder surged and his chest cramped. Please… No… Just leave. Leave him here, alone, until he exploded and ruined Vox’s floor and his own dignity in one fell swoop… Just, please, not that!

It had made him uncomfortable enough the one time he’d done it when he’d CHOSEN to. When he’d thought he’d WANTED it— Because he was SUPPOSED to want it, right?— It had still felt so wrong and gross, and he’d wanted to stop immediately!

Now, when not a single part of him even THOUGHT it wanted it, when he HATED Valentino, when he was powerless and trapped, when he knew Valentino WOULDN’T stop no matter what he said or did…

His ears flattened. He backed himself against the chair as much as he could.

“I just want to know what the big deal is,” Valentino said. “Some nights, Vox makes me put on a red suit and a monocle. And, he says ‘Now, talk like a radio announcer’. Did you know that?”

Alastor would have preferred NOT to know that.

“Too bad we don’t have a contract…” Valentino said. “How I’d love to play with you, especially when you’re so full…”

Alastor released a shaky breath. Valentino couldn’t force him without a soul contract in place. He was okay… It wouldn’t happen… It wouldn’t happen…

“…Valentino,” he said, still in his pleasant tone but with radio-static fraying at the edges, “Please.”

The word please was what finally did it.

Valentino blinked, startled. He clicked his tongue, walked behind the chair, and unfastened the first knot. “Oh, you beg so sweetly,” he murmured.

Alastor didn’t respond— Because for once, he had bigger concerns.

Valentino’s long nails clicked rhythmically as he worked at the binds, tugging each knot loose with exaggerated slowness. Alastor sat unnaturally still, posture rigid, forcing every fiber of his body to behave. His smile remained in place, elegant and unbothered, but the tightness around his eyes betrayed the strain.

“Honestly,” Valentino drawled, “If I had known this was all it took to hear you say please, I would’ve tried this ages ago.”

“You’ll find I can be quite cooperative,” Alastor replied, “When the situation warrants it.”

“Mm-hm.”

Another knot came undone. Alastor inhaled sharply through his nose, the sound shifting slightly into faint crackling static. He did not dare move more than a fraction, every shift of his weight was a calculated risk.

Valentino stepped around him, studying him openly now that the worst of the bindings were undone.

“You’re really holding it together, huh?” he mused. “Never thought I’d see the day the Radio Demon looked… So exquisite.”

“I am maintaining perfect composure,” Alastor insisted, though the stiffness in his tone and the way his fingers twitched as circulation returned said otherwise.

Valentino smirked. “Sure you are. Stand up.”

Alastor rose carefully, every muscle tightening in unison. It was a difficult balancing act— One wrong shift, and dignity could slip through his fingers like sand. He kept his smile intact by sheer force of will.

The room swayed slightly as he found his footing. The bindings had left faint impressions on his wrists, but he ignored them, instead focusing entirely on maintaining control.

Valentino watched him the way a cat watches a glass teetering on the edge of a table.

“You know,” Valentino said, lighting a cigarette, “I could make this real easy for you. Show you where the nearest bathroom is.”

“That,” Alastor said through a warm, forced chuckle, “Would be appreciated.”

“But…” Valentino exhaled a slow plume of smoke. “I want something in return.”

Alastor’s eyes flicked to him sharply, crimson light blinking through the pupils like a warning signal. “This hardly seems the time for negotiation.”

“Oh, I think it’s exactly the time. ’Cause you don’t look like you have many options, sweetheart.”

Alastor’s smile trembled only slightly. “You mistake urgency for leverage.”

“Do I?” Valentino leaned closer, smoke curling between them. “You look like you’re one good poke away from losing it.”

Alastor’s entire frame went still. Then— Slowly— He leaned forwards, his shadow stretching behind him in a subtle ripple. “I am giving you the rare opportunity,” he said quietly, pleasant as a radio host introducing a lullaby, “To taunt me without consequence. But if you insist on complicating matters…” Static hissed beneath his voice. “…There will be consequences.”

Valentino paused, smile faltering.

For a moment, they stared at each other— One demon fighting to keep still, the other reevaluating how far to push him.

Finally, Valentino raised his hands in surrender. “Alright, alright—Geez. Lighten up.” He gestured towards the hallway. “Bathroom’s down the corridor, third door on the left. Let’s go before you explode or whatever.”

Alastor exhaled, relief flickering across his face so fleetingly that only someone watching closely— Like Valentino— Would catch it. He straightened, nodding once. “Thank you.”

And with as much grace as he could muster, he began walking toward the door, each step measured, careful… And faster than his usual leisurely stroll.

Valentino followed, grinning; “You’re welcome. Now, let’s go see what all your fussing has been about.”

Wait… See?! Oh, fuck. Did that mean what he thought it did?!

It did.

Not only did Valentino follow him TO the bathroom, he followed him IN.

“You know,” Valentino said cheerfully. “I never pegged you as being so shy.” Then, he laughed and repeated the word “pegged” to himself.

Alastor, however, was more focused on the word “shy,” which hit like an ice pick.

Alastor’s hand tightened on his zipper, frozen and unwilling to pull it down even though his body was screaming that he NEEDED to. His knuckles were ghost-white. His heart throbbed with sharp, humiliating awareness. The presence of another person in the room made the rest impossible, a wall of tension slamming into place.

“Valentino,” Alastor said smoothly, but with noticeable venom. Like a snake wrapped in silk. “Your presence is as unnecessary as it is unwanted.”

“Ohoho, now that’s the tone I missed.” Valentino leaned against the wall and grinned “Funny seein’ you all quiet in here. You’re usually so… Loud. You can’t go at ALL, can you? You’re too scared to even whip your dick out…”

Alastor’s eye twitched. His radio static flared faintly, betraying the agitation he tried so hard to smother. He shut his eyes and pulled down his zipper, aiming in the vain hope that SOMETHING might come out, easing the pressure pangs coiling inside him.

Of course, it didn’t.

And of course, Valentino’s eyes were focused exactly where Alastor DIDN’T want them.

“Hm. Nice,” Valentino said. “Thought it’d be redder, like a deer.”

Alastor covered up as much as he could. Truth be told, directly after he’d died and arrived in Hell, that had been one of the things he’d been keen to check once he had the privacy to inspect his new form. It embarrassed him enough that he’d sprouted antlers, designating the ridiculous way he’d died. And, his toes had been replaced with hooves, which threw off his balance a bit. It would have been quite discomforting if his private areas had changed to reflect it as well.

But, thankfully his… Posterior was normal; No tail had appeared there to make fitting suits difficult. And his member was almost exactly what he was used to, it had just taken on the grey tone the rest of his body had gained in death. The hair had also changed, still curly but colored red instead of brown.

Satisfied that no further humiliating marks of his undignified death were present in his form, he hadn’t really thought about it much since then. Everything about his urinary system worked— Or, rather, DIDN’T work— Exactly as it had when he was alive, which was the only functional matter he ever concerned himself with.

Naturally, Valentino was interested in the appendage’s OTHER purpose. “How much action does it get down here? If no one’s touching that, it’s a damn sha—“

“I am trying to relieve myself,” Alastor interrupted, trying not to shudder. If Valentino even TRIED to lay a hand on him there... Alastor wouldn’t even care about his plan anymore, he’d break his deal with Vox to give this demon what he deserved. He wasn’t tied down anymore. He’d fight back with everything he had. “That is all I require.”

“Oh, right. Right,” Valentino said. He went quiet for a second, but only a second. “Some people think deer dicks are medicinal.”

“Valentino.”

“No, I’m serious. Supposed to help with your bones… Oh, and blood flow, and kidney function— Looks like yours isn’t helping much with that, though, is it?”

This went beyond anything Alastor had ever endured. The wound in his chest from battling Adam didn’t scorch him as badly as the bright, hot humiliation of this moment was. He knew that, no matter how powerful he was, Hell was still going to feel like HELL sometimes, but this was just—

His bladder knotted up on itself, sharp tingles skittering along his sides as the pressure surged against his opening with all its might, but couldn’t quite make it beyond the barrier. When he looked down, he could see a distinct curve where his liquids were trapped. He felt compelled to press on it, to see if he could SQUEEZE the fluid out of himself and be freed from all this torture.

But, he needed one hand to keep himself aimed in case something finally decided to release, and the other to TRY to shield Valentino’s view of his private area.

His ears flattened. His smile twitched.

“Aww. Somethin’ got you stuck?” Valentino asked.

Silence.

“You look SO good this way, baby,” Valentino said. “You really, really need it, don’t you? All that pressure, wouldn’t it feel good to piss it out? You have to SO bad, right? But, you can’t. No matter what. You’re going to keep holding it and squirming… You don’t even have a choice. Oh, will you beg? Beg me to give you privacy?”

Alastor’s entire body stiffened like a tuning fork struck too hard. The urge to strangle Valentino where he stood clashed violently with the mortifying awareness that he could do absolutely nothing until his bladder was empty and he’d regained his full range of motion.

He despised this. He despised being cornered. He despised being seen.

Especially like this.

“Leave,” Alastor said softly, dangerously. “Now.”

“Or what?” Valentino prodded. “You gonna force me out? ’Cause you can’t do much right now.”

The room filled with static, lights flickering.

Valentino paused—but only for a heartbeat. “Ooh. Touched a nerve.”

Alastor’s smile widened, too sharp, too strained. “You are testing the limits of my restraint.”

“And you,” Valentino replied, “Are makin’ this the highlight of my week. I wish I had a camera, you know how much people would pay to see this? Especially with the free advertisement Vox gave us…”

Alastor strained his lower muscles so hard that he released an involuntary grunt. ‘Come ON,’ he thought. ‘This will be over once you manage to—‘

“So tell me,” Valentino said, “Is it stage fright? Trauma? Or are you just scared your uh, equipment doesn’t live up to expectations? Because, listen honey,” he whistled. “It surpasses them.”

Alastor’s entire body went rigid with mortified disbelief. Static crackled violently. “Valentino,” he said with the politest homicidal restraint Hell had ever known, “I assure you, I am in no mood for commentary.”

The bathroom felt suddenly too small. The pressure to perform a supposedly simple biological function while another demon— Especially THIS one— Loitered beside him made Alastor’s pulse quicken. His muscles refused to relax under scrutiny.

The silence stretched, thick and suffocating.

Valentino only smiled. “You know,” he said, “I could watch this performance all day.”

‘And that,’ Alastor thought bleakly, ‘Is the problem.’

He couldn’t use brute force. Couldn’t intimidate Valentino away without inviting Vox’s attention and breaking their deal. Couldn’t continue to stand frozen in a bathroom for an hour, waiting for the pimp to lose interest.

This was becoming a standoff.

All over something so absurdly trivial.

His composure faltered by one degree. One. A whisper almost lost behind the crackle of static: “…Please. Leave.”

A slow, quiet chuckle rolled through the room.“Ohhh,” Valentino murmured. “Was that a beg I just heard?”

“It was a request. A perfectly reasonable one, at that.”

“I think it was a beg…”

“It would be wise,” Alastor said in a low, controlled tone, “To remove yourself before I ensure that no one will ever find enough of you to glue back together.”

“Threats, threats, threats. You’re hot when you’re cornered. Beg a LITTLE more, and maybe I’ll give you some private-time.”

Alastor inhaled slowly through his nose. He needed Valentino gone. Needed calm. Needed quiet. The longer this went on, the more impossible the original task became. His condition didn’t just resist interruptions— It shut down entirely the moment another presence lingered.

And Valentino was nothing if not a lingering presence.

He would NOT beg… Instead, he lifted his chin. “If you insist on spectating, do continue. But understand that your existence becomes more tenuous by the second.”

Valentino laughed. “Oh, this is better than TV.”

Alastor’s smile twitched. He was going to kill him. Not now. Not here. Not until his plan was finished. But later. Slowly.

For now, though, he was stuck—held hostage by a bodily function he couldn’t perform, and an enemy who refused to leave.

Valentino lounged against the sink like he owned the place, neon glow reflecting in the cracked mirror behind him.

Alastor stood perfectly still at the toilet, his static danced off of him as he seethed. “You’re still here,” he said lightly, though the strain behind the words was palpable.

“Aww, don’t act surprised.” Valentino stretched lazily. “You didn’t really expect me to leave when you’re this fun to watch, did you?”

Alastor’s smile didn’t waver, but the corners spasmed. “There is nothing to watch. I assure you. We’ve established that I can’t perform.”

“Oh yes, but that just gives me something even BETTER to watch,” Valentino tilted his head, giving Alastor a slow, knowing once–over. “So tense… And look, you even have a bladder-bulge. Delicious.” He moved closer, hovered a hand over the straining protrusion in Alastor’s middle.

“Don’t touch me!” Alastor exclaimed. The static in the room deepened. “No!”

Valentino did it anyway, laying his hand flatly against Alastor’s searing bladder. Alastor’s earlier assumption that squeezing it would lead to relief was instantly proven very, VERY wrong. The added weight against it made the urgency spike, but rather than the fluid spraying out, it stayed locked right where it was, pain dancing along all of Alastor’s raw nerves.

Worse than the pain was the touch itself. He’d said ‘no’. He’d said ‘don’t’. He hated being touched by all but a very select few— And, even then, permission had to be granted first. He tensed, his muscles tightening around his bladder, but even that agony wasn’t as bad as the sense that he was being violated.

Valentino finally let go and stepped back, humming to himself. “WOW, you are FULL… Feels like a bowling ball wedged in there. And were those some whimpers I heard?”

“Get. Out.”

Valentino clicked his tongue. “If you really want me gone…” His grin was predatory, pleased with itself. “You could beg a bit more.”

Alastor’s expression froze. “…What?”

“You heard me,” Valentino said, voice dropping smugly. “Beg.”

The word hung in the air like a spark in dry tinder.

Alastor straightened to his full height, posture immaculate and murderous. “I do not ‘beg.’”

Valentino snorted. “Then, you’re fucked.” He leaned one shoulder against a wall. “Because right now? You’re stuck. And I know it. And you know it.” He tapped the wall with a manicured finger. “You’re not doing anything until I walk out.”

Alastor’s smile widened into something so sharp it was almost painful to look at. “Careful, Valentino. You are approaching a line you cannot uncross.”

“Sweetheart, I dove across that line when I felt up your bladder.”

The Radio Demon’s eye twitched— A minute, nearly imperceptible motion, but enough to make Valentino grin wider.

“You know,” Valentino continued lightly, “Overlords aren’t supposed to have weaknesses. Kinda ruins the mystique.” He lifted a brow. “Yet, you freeze up over something this small…” His laugh was low, cruel, echoing off the tiles.

Alastor didn’t move, didn’t breathe— His entire being went still, like a predator calculating the exact moment to strike. “Leave,” he said softly, dangerously. “Or I will peel you apart layer by layer until even your cells begs for mercy.”

Valentino waved a hand. “Empty threats. If you were gonna tear me up, you’d have done it before.” He stepped closer again, invading personal space just to watch Alastor tense. “All you gotta do is ask nicely. Just a bit of begging. That’s it.”

Alastor’s voice dropped to a low, resonant growl. “You will not get that satisfaction.”

Valentino shrugged, pleased. “Then I guess I’m staying.”

Silence flooded the room— Thick, oppressive, electric.

Alastor weighed his options. Brute force. Intimidation. Bribery. A deal. Manipulation.

But Valentino wasn’t here for profit or power— He was here because something about Alastor’s predicament was far, far too entertaining for him. And entertainment was a much harder currency to counter.

Alastor inhaled, smoothing his composure back into place, straightening his tie with immaculate precision. Then, slowly, deliberately, he turned to face Valentino with a smile that could fracture diamond. “Very well,” he said softly. “If it is a request you want…”

Valentino leaned in, gleeful. “Go on.”

Alastor paused— Just long enough to let Valentino think he’d won something— Then the Radio Demon’s grin split wide, glowing with malevolent delight. “…I shall ask you courteously.” His voice boomed, warped with radio distortion, a hundred layered tones vibrating the walls; “REMOVE YOURSELF FROM MY PRESENCE.”

The lights exploded overhead. The mirror cracked. Valentino stumbled back instinctively.

For a moment, neither spoke. Then Valentino steadied himself, brushing glass off his jacket. “Nope. Try again,” he taunted. “BEG. Tell me how bad you need to go! How desperate you are for your precious privacy!”

Alastor’s eyes narrowed imperceptibly. “You are a remarkably disgusting creature, aren’t you?”

“Well, I’d say I’m more… Consistent. You want something? Prove it. Otherwise, I’m not going anywhere.”

Alastor didn’t respond at first. He stood at the toilet, straining for even a few drops of relief, smile brittle as antique porcelain. The room hummed with static— Residual, involuntary.

Valentino took a step closer. “You can keep whining,” he said lightly, “But, we both know how this ends.”

Alastor inhaled slowly. The smile stayed. But, his eyes— Usually gleaming with mischief— Dimmed just slightly. He still had not recovered from the pressure of Valentino’s hand against his bladder. The stone it was made of was splitting, just enough to amplify the miserable need, but not enough to let anything seep out.

His sides ached, his back was starting to shudder. He was so filled with urgency that there was little room left in him for anything else. It was a struggle to keep any thought in his mind, other than the frantic repetition of ‘I need to go!’

Valentino noticed. His grin sharpened. “That’s it,” he whispered. “Right there. That little crack. You’re not invincible. You’re not untouchable. You’re just—”

“Please.” The word came out quiet. Too quiet.

Valentino blinked. “…What was that?”

Alastor didn’t repeat himself. He stood as still as he could, face angled downward so his eyes were shadowed by his hair. The static in the air faltered, broke, and faded.

“Are you…” Valentino stepped closer, incredulous. “Are you giving up?”

“No.” Alastor’s voice was smooth— Too smooth. A practiced control barely masking something frayed. “I am asking you to stop.”

“Where’s the fight?” Valentino taunted. “Where’s the big scary Radio Demon?“

Alastor didn’t look up. Then, in a voice so soft it almost didn’t sound like him: “…Please.”

It wasn’t just humiliation. It wasn’t just desperation. It was something rawer, quieter. A single word forced through decades of pride, dignity, and ironclad self-possession.

Valentino stared at him, stunned.

Alastor continued, still not meeting his eyes; “… Please. Please. I am asking for my privacy. Nothing more.”

Valentino opened his mouth, closed it, then tried again.

“Please,” Alastor murmured. “Please. I need… I can’t…”

And suddenly, the situation wasn’t just a LITTLE hot anymore. Valentino was seeing an overlord— One of Hell’s most composed, untouchable figures— Step down from the stage and completely come apart in the face of an extreme, uncontrollable need that his own body wouldn’t let him satisfy. That VALENTINO could stop him from satisfying.

“Go on…” Valentino said softly.

Alastor’s ears flattened. How was that still not enough?! He’d begged! “I— Please! It’s painful!”

“How painful, sweetheart? How does it feel?”

“I feel… I feel like… Like I’m going to… To explode. Please. The pressure… It’s moving. It’s climbing up my back. If I don’t… If I don’t release I may become ill!”

“Mmm, that bad?” Valentino asked. “You feel it everywhere? It’s making you sick?”

“Yes! Just, leave so I may finally—“

Valentino DIDN’T leave. No. Of course not. He’d humiliated Alastor, he’d violated him with his words and his touches, and he’d made him beg. But, that still wasn’t enough.

All FOUR of Valentino’s hands gripped Alastor’s midsection and squeezed with the force of someone trying to shove all his organs up and out through his mouth. Red, blazing agony soared through him and all of his energy immediately directed itself towards keeping his smile in place.

“Then, why don’t you go for me?” Valentino murmured, crushing Alastor even tighter. His hands got firmer and firmer, until—

A confusing swirl of overwhelming emotions rushed over Alastor. Relief mixed with pain, mixed with humiliation, mixed with rage.

He was peeing. Oh, fuck, he was peeing so much. It was a waterfall. A typhoon. Wave after wave of long-held liquid sprayed the toilet in an angry gush, his bladder muscles giving out under Valentino’s aggressive squishing. It was so loud that Alastor’s ears rang and, still pinned in place, he couldn’t adjust his aim enough to hit the side of the bowl and quiet it down.

It felt… Oh, it felt good… It felt terrible… It felt so embarrassing that he wished he’d just die again… It felt… “Ahhhhhhhhhhh…” a moan of blissful satisfaction flew from his lips before he could stop it, before he could even process it. He was urinating, and it was such a relief…

Valentino shuddered behind him, letting out a small moan of his own. Alastor couldn’t even begin to understand what THAT was about before the disgusting demon commented; “Geez, you DID have to piss… Look at you go. You ain’t even slowing down… And it sounds like a fire hydrant.”

Alastor’s ears pressed even harder against his head. It DID sound like a fire hydrant, and that was the problem! Valentino was listening to it! He was talking about it! He was watching!

And he was TOUCHING Alastor as he did something so very personal and private, after Alastor had literally BEGGED to be left alone.

Alastor released a strange, scratchy noise. Partway between a sigh of relief and a whimper of agony.

“That’s it, Alastor… Let me see what was making you so miserable.”

‘YOU are what was making me so miserable!’ Alastor thought. ‘And you STILL are!’

Valentino pressed down more firmly on Alastor’s abdomen, causing him to void even more strongly. “Fuck, look at you! It’s like squeezing a sponge…”

Alastor clenched his eyes closed, but removing the visual did little to soften the humiliation of the moment.

Valentino had stopped squeezing him, had moved on to rubbing small circles around Alastor’s bladder. He supposed maybe that was supposed to feel GOOD? But, it didn’t. It was revolting. He never wanted to feel Valentino’s hands on him again!

But… He did. He felt one of them in… In the spot where he least wanted to ever feel ANY hand that wasn’t his own. Valentino had grabbed his… Most personal spot, and given IT a squeeze, both blocking off Alastor’s flow and filling him with such a heavy sense of revulsion that he felt bile rising in his throat. No, please, don’t let him vomit in front of this sick creep on top of everything else that had already happened to him.

He tried to force Valentino’s hand away, but the grip only tightened, hard enough to make Alastor actually YELP from the pain, and for a screech of radio feedback to erupt from him as well.

Alastor’s bladder was burning, furious at having its release stopped, and Alastor was even more furious at HIMSELF, that in the midst of this awful defilement, his need for relief still mattered enough to register so prominently.

‘Do not touch me. Do not touch me. DO NOT FUCKING TOUCH ME!’ Alastor screamed in his mind, and his radio static was going completely ballistic. So high that it even hurt his own ears. So loud that it almost blocked out his thundering heartbeat. ‘STOP. NO.’

And then, when what came out was an utterly pathetic whimper of “I’m n—not finished yet…” Alastor wished that Hell would just swallow him up.

“You were being too loud, honey,” Valentino purred.

Alastor shook so hard that he thought he’d collapse.

“You need to learn to be quieter… Ten second penalty.” Valentino counted down slowly, then FINALLY removed his hand from Alastor’s personal area, and mashed down on his bladder once again. He had to squeeze much harder this time, as the Radio Demon was now tenser than ever, but eventually the stream resumed.

When, finally, Alastor’s stream tapered off, Valentino whistled. “Wooow, you might have just set a record. You ARE done, right? I don’t need to squeeze you again?”

Alastor said nothing. He just shoved himself back into his pants and zipped them, pulling his coat around himself.

For so long, he’d been worried about what VOX would do if he discovered his problem. And, Vox had done precisely what Alastor had predicted; He’d laughed, he’d taunted him, he’d given him a hard time as he tried to go, and he’d run to his cameras to tell all of Hell about it.

And all of that was devastating for Alastor’s reputation, but at least Vox had been predictable. At least it had all been a possibility Alastor had kept at the back of his mind, so it was less of a shock to his system when it all came to pass. He still didn’t know what to DO about the blow dealt to everyone’s perception of him, but he wasn’t left reeling and shaking from the event itself.

This was different.

He had never put any thought into how the other Vees would behave. He had never ONCE imagined Valentino would be something to worry about.

But, he was.

Alastor felt completely violated. His control had been stolen from him, he’d been humiliated, he’d been touched, he’d been made to beg…

When Valentino tied him back into his chair, Alastor was silent, his grin pasted on. He wanted a bath, but knew better than to ask. Valentino might try to GIVE him one, rather than allow him to clean off by himself.

Once he was alone, Alastor admitted something to himself; He wanted Charlie. She would be able to say something to make these feelings go away. He wanted Rosie, having his ears scratched would settle the pounding of his heart.

He… Needed others. Kind of. Maybe. Just a little.

What he most wanted was his mother. He wanted to be in her arms, where he always knew he would be safe. He wanted to snuggle against her, as she told him everything would be okay, and that none of what had just happened was his fault. He just… Wanted his mother… He NEEDED his mother. He needed that comfort, that gentle, maternal warmth. He needed it from SOMEWHERE.

After his plan was completed and he was off Rosie’s leash, he decided that they would still remain… Companions. He hadn’t been certain of that until this moment, but now he knew.

Alastor decided he would NEVER ask Valentino to untie him after that last incident. Requesting help getting out of the chair was bad. Vox taunting him behind the door and cackling while he struggled to go was worse, but being TOUCHED, having his body manipulated, having Valentino enjoy it so much…
